- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.**
  - `temporaryEquityClaims` mirrors Stage A's `pickTemporaryEquity` admission (`stageA/validate.ts:251-268`): total, else parent + NCI, else the observed part, with "the other part undisclosed" in the basis. A conflict or a refused field returns `refusal`.
  - valueCompany folds the claims at its TOP, right after the model-eligibility return and before the `noncontrollingOwnershipUnresolved` gate and the E-5 block (`valuation.ts:5807`, E-5 `:5815-5848`), so E-5 sees the redeemable floor: `preferred = (isNum(p) ? p : 0) + redeemablePreferred + unsplit` and, when `redeemableNoncontrolling > 0` and `isNum(mi)`, `minorityInterest = mi + redeemableNoncontrolling`. When `mi` is null, `minorityInterest` stays null (pre-flight I2: a null book is not a zero claim; `bridgeToPerShare`'s null-as-zero convention never reaches the redeemable floor), the redeemable NCI is carried beside it, and E-5's null-book branch (`valuation.ts:5827-5833`) fires on `!isNum(mi) && (noncontrollingIncomeSigned || redeemableNoncontrolling > 0)`, its reason naming "a redeemable noncontrolling interest of {millions(redeemableNoncontrolling)} filed in temporary equity" beside the unavailable book value. It records `bridge.temporaryEquity` and pushes one note naming "temporary equity attributable to the parent" and "redeemable noncontrolling interests". When `redeemablePreferred + unsplit > 0`, the note adds the residual sentence: "This caption can also hold redeemable common shares (for example shares subject to possible redemption or an ESOP put), which may already be in the diluted share count; the filed facts do not separate them from redeemable preferred, so such shares would be counted both as a claim and in the divisor."
  - A refusal sets the per share null with the warn gap. After `runDcf`, when `unsplit > 0` and `dcf.bridge.noncontrollingDeductionBasis === "earnings-share"`, the per share (and `equityValue`) is null with the same gap.
  - `unresolvedCashOwnership` and the two common-allocation checks treat a positive admitted claim like `preferredStock` / `minorityInterest` (the row carries the three fields after `toMultiplesBalance`).
  - The multiples EV and the own-history EV add `redeemablePreferred + redeemableNoncontrolling + unsplit`; a refusal withholds the EV.
  - `temporaryEquityUnknown` (Interfaces) adds no claim and withholds nothing; it raises the one warn gap.

**Files:**
- Modify `src/pipeline/stageB/netDebt.ts:126` at B13 (`:114-131` at `main`) (add `operatingLeaseOutOfDebt` beside `operatingLeaseScopeUnproven`).
- Modify `src/pipeline/stageB/valuation.ts:724` (`DcfAssumptionInputs` gains `includeLeasesInEv?: boolean`), `:186-232` (`establishedSalesToCapital` at `:194`, per-row rule; export it so the history is testable without a sub-0.5 current ratio: the basis prints the history only when the floor binds), `:2128-2175` (DCF `icDebt`; the FCX-1 partial-coverage path is unchanged; the s2c basis names the lease removed or kept; the `ic <= 0` branch at `:2135-2143` gains I6's history-or-withhold rule).
- Modify `src/pipeline/stageB/returns.ts:1463-1479` (ROIC requires the proof).
- Modify `src/pipeline/compute.ts:2159` (no new config read: pass the existing `evIncludeLeases` into `dcfInputs`).
- Re-pin `tests/stageB.growth.returns.capital.test.ts:1415-1451`: its fixture is an unproven row; make it proven with `capitalLeaseObligations` equal to the lease and say why (FIN-303-V1). Extend `tests/lease.partialOperating.test.ts` with the unproven ROIC case.
- Test: `tests/stageB.leaseBasisCapital.audit20261027.test.ts`.

**Interfaces:**
- Produces `export function operatingLeaseOutOfDebt(row: LeaseDebtInputs & { shortTermDebt?: number | null; longTermDebt?: number | null }, includeLeases: boolean): { amount: number; note: string | null }`. `amount` is the operating slice when `!includeLeases`, `0 < op <= totalDebt` and `operatingLeaseScopeUnproven(row) === null`; otherwise 0, with `note` set to the unproven reason.
- Produces `DcfAssumptionInputs.includeLeasesInEv?: boolean` (absent is false, the default switch).
- Produces `export function establishedSalesToCapital(incomeHistory: DcfIncomeRow[], capitalHistory: DcfBalanceRow[] | null, lease: { fcx1Excluded: boolean; includeLeasesInEv: boolean; currentRowExcludesLease: boolean }): { ratio: number | null; basis: string }`. One median is on one lease basis, the current balance's (pre-flight minor):
  - `fcx1Excluded` is `main`'s `leaseExcluded` flag (every row subtracts its operating lease, a missing one skips the row), unchanged;
  - else, when `includeLeasesInEv` is set or the current row keeps its lease (`currentRowExcludesLease === false`, its scope unproven), every row keeps its debt as reported (one lease-inclusive basis);
  - else each row subtracts `operatingLeaseOutOfDebt(row, false).amount`, and a row carrying a positive operating lease that the predicate does not prove inside its total debt (its `note` is set) is left out of the median with a note naming the date and the reason; it is never kept lease-inclusive beside lease-excluded years.
  - The only caller is `buildDcfAssumptions`. **R1a rebuilds its `salesToCapitalUsableDates` export on this signature** (R1 plan, cross-batch; pre-flight I11 (a)): its "under both lease bases" enumerates this lease object's states, and its usable rows exclude the two new unusable kinds, a refused temporary-equity year (Task 3) and a row left out for an unproven lease scope (this task).
- **Lease-emptied capital (pre-flight I6).** When the current invested capital on this lease basis is ≤ 0, `buildDcfAssumptions` takes `establishedSalesToCapital(…, same lease object).ratio` when it is non-null (at least `S2C_HISTORY_MIN_YEARS` = 3 usable years and A-2's ratio rules), within the house cap, with a basis saying invested capital is not positive once the operating lease leaves debt and naming the history. Otherwise it returns the critical `valuation.dcf.salesToCapital` gap, its reason naming invested capital (`millions()`) and the lease basis. It never recomputes on the lease-inclusive (mixed) basis.
  - **Long-term investments in this branch (re-check minor; OD-6).** A filed long-term investment is still added back in the bridge, and `nonOperatingInvestments` is recorded whenever `lti > 0`, as OD-6 says for every case.
  - This branch runs first when the lease subtraction makes `ic` itself ≤ 0. Task 5's house-cap rule applies only when `ic > 0` and `ic − lti ≤ 0`, because its reason (the holding empties a positive operating capital) is false when the capital is already non-positive.

**Interfaces:**
- Produces `DebtLikeObligations` gaining OPTIONAL members `pension?: FiledObligation | null; lossContingencies?: FiledObligation | null; contingentConsideration?: FiledObligation | null` (optional so the existing E-7 literals in `tests/stageB.debtLikeObligations.audit20261022.test.ts` and any other `DebtLikeObligations` literal still type-check; `debtLikeObligationsAt` always sets all three).
- Produces `interface TagChoice { total: string[]; parts: string[]; currentAloneIsWhole?: boolean }`.
- Produces `const PENSION_FAMILIES: readonly TagChoice[]` (tried in order (a), (b), (c) per OD-4; family (c) is two choices summed: pension plus OPEB). The look-back is **date first** (pre-flight minor): `candidateDates` are walked newest first, and at each date the families are tried in order; the first date at which any family has a filed value wins, and a filed zero at that date ends the look-back.
- Produces `ValuationBundleInputs.rateRegulatedUtility?: boolean` (absent is false) and manifest field `valuation.dcf.debtLikeObligations.rateRegulated` (info), raised when a utility's pension and OPEB deficit is read and not deducted (pre-flight I7).
- Produces `export const RATE_REGULATED_UTILITY_SIC: ReadonlySet<number>` in `src/edgar/sic.ts` (4911, 4923, 4924, 4931, 4932, 4941).
- Produces `ValuationBundleInputs.sicEstablished?: boolean` (absent is false) and the manifest field `valuation.dcf.debtLikeObligations.sicUnknown` (info). The gap is raised only when `sicEstablished` is not true AND a pension and OPEB line is deducted (addendum 2, item 4).
- Consumes the existing look-back (`candidateDates`, `filedZero`, `annualBalanceDates`).

### Task 7: A successor with no annual report of its own bears its predecessor's filed obligations (FIN-403)

**Files:**
- Modify `src/pipeline/stageB/debtLikeObligations.ts:8-12` (the "only the issuer's own facts" sentence gains the successor case) and append `successorObligationsAt`.
- Modify `src/pipeline/compute.ts:3966-3968` (call `successorObligationsAt` with `bundle.edgar.predecessor` and `successorContinuity(ownFacts, predecessor.facts) !== null`; push the info gap when a predecessor is present but continuity is not established).
- Test: `tests/stageB.successorObligations.audit20261027.test.ts`.

**Interfaces:**
- Produces `export function successorObligationsAt(own: CompanyFacts | null | undefined, predecessor: { facts: CompanyFacts; cik10: string; continuity: boolean } | null, date: string | null, unit: string | null, observedAt: string): DebtLikeObligations | null`.
  - The merge is PER FAMILY (asset retirement, environmental, settlement assets, pension, loss contingencies, contingent consideration): each member takes the successor's own reading when it is non-null.
  - A member the successor does not tag takes the predecessor's reading only when `continuity` holds AND the successor's own facts hold no annual balance (`10-K`/`20-F`/`40-F` `Assets`) dated on or before `date` (an annual report of its own ends the predecessor read for every family). Each predecessor `basis` is prefixed "predecessor (CIK {cik10}, one reporting entity under B-36): ". A whole-object rule (own result wins if any family is non-null) would drop the predecessor's pension or ARO whenever the successor's first 10-Q tags one unrelated family.
  - `settlementAssets` follows the retirement obligation it offsets: read from the same facts (own or predecessor) as `assetRetirement`.

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  ```ts
  export interface DivisorRow { date: string; basic: number | null; diluted: number | null; netIncome: number | null;
    continuingIncome?: number | null; conversionShares?: number | null }
  export function awardDilutedDivisor(row: DivisorRow, donors: readonly DivisorRow[]):
    { shares: number | null; basis: "filed" | "loss-period-substituted" | "loss-period-basic"; donorDate: string | null; note: string | null }
  ```
  A loss row is `diluted <= basic + 1` with `continuingIncome ?? netIncome` below 0. The donor is the newest of `donors` with positive income and `diluted − basic − (conversionShares ?? 0) > 0`. Task 11 (B4b) supplies `conversionShares`; until then it is undefined.
- Produces manifest field `valuation.perShare.lossPeriodDivisor` (info).

The TTM stays profitable (45 M × 3 − 40 M = 95 M), so no unprofitable overlay.

**Interfaces:**
- Produces `export const BRIDGE_METHOD_SINCE_SPEC_VERSION = "1.50.0"` (pre-flight I8: the REPORT_SPEC_VERSION Task 16 introduces, next after the preceding merge; renumbered at merge together with it, then FROZEN). It sits above B3's `PAYLOAD_RECORD_CORRECTION_SPEC` "1.45.0" and B13's `EDGAR_DERIVED_LINES_SINCE_SPEC_VERSION` "1.49.0"; the plan's earlier "1.45.0" equalled B3's cutoff, so reports made at 1.45.0-1.49.0 on the old bridge method would have shown no note. The rule must NOT compare against `REPORT_SPEC_VERSION` itself: B4b, B5 and later batches move that constant, and a report made under this batch's method would then be marked legacy. Task 16's stamp test asserts `specVersionBefore(REPORT_SPEC_VERSION, BRIDGE_METHOD_SINCE_SPEC_VERSION) === false`.
- Produces `export function withLegacyBridgeMethod(report: Report): Report`. Pure; returns a new object; never mutates its input; idempotent (a report that already carries a line is returned unchanged for that line, because the chain runs twice on some paths: `parseStoredReportWithSafety` and then the export). **Each clause compares the report's spec with its own part's cutoff, and there is no module-wide early return** (the pattern of B5's `legacyValuationNotes.ts`): B4a's clauses use `BRIDGE_METHOD_SINCE_SPEC_VERSION`, and Task 15b adds B4b's clauses under `BRIDGE_READINGS_SINCE_SPEC_VERSION`, so a report built between the two merges still reaches B4b's.
- **Scoped to the reports it is true for (pre-flight minor; the B13 Task 10b lesson: never fire on a report the change did not touch).** Each clause fires only when a stored-byte detector shows that the old method touched that report. Where no stored byte can show it, the clause is worded as a condition ("if the issuer filed any …"), never asserted. No clause fires unless `report.fairValue?.method` is `"fcff-dcf"` or `"excess-return"` with `status === "available"` (or, for the REIT note, `report.routeMetrics?.route === "reit"`).
  - **Route scope (re-check).** The claims, E-7 and lease clauses fire only when `report.fairValue.method === "fcff-dcf"`. The excess-return route values common book equity: it has no E-7 bridge and no sales-to-capital, and only Task 9's divisor reaches it. So an excess-return report can carry only the loss-quarter divisor clause.
  - The lease detector requires a stored sales-to-capital assumption basis in `valuation.dcf.assumptions[]` that does not say the lease was removed. With no such basis stored, the clause is absent, never vacuously true.
  - (a) It appends one line to `fairValue.basis` (rendered by `markdown.ts:540`, `printHtml.ts:494` at B13, `sections.tsx:1000`), after any line B13's wrapper appended: "Valued under the equity-bridge method before report spec {BRIDGE_METHOD_SINCE_SPEC_VERSION}: {clauses}. Re-run the report for the current method." The clauses, in this order, each with its detector:
    - **claims** (no stored trace, so conditional): "the bridge did not read temporary-equity (redeemable) claims, pension and OPEB deficits, loss-contingency accruals or contingent consideration, so any the issuer filed were not deducted";
    - **E-7 before tax** (detector: some `report.valuation.dcf.bridge.nonOperatingComponents[].label` contains "(filed, before tax", the E-7 label, D-104): "it deducted the filed retirement and environmental obligations shown in its bridge at their full amount, before the tax relief a settlement earns". Absent otherwise, because a report made before D-104, or one whose issuer tagged no such obligation, deducted none, and the note must not say it did (P1, D-67);
    - **lease basis** (detector: the stored EV basis, `valuation.multiplesBasis` or `valuation.marketValueScope.enterpriseValueBasis`, names an operating-lease liability left out of net debt under the D-25 default, and the stored sales-to-capital assumption basis in `valuation.dcf.assumptions[]` does not say it was removed): "sales-to-capital kept the {millions(amount)} operating lease the EV left out in invested capital";
    - **loss-quarter divisor** (detector: the stored divisor note or diluted-shares assumption basis shows the divisor period's diluted count at or below its basic count beside a loss; otherwise conditional): "if the divisor period was a loss quarter, its divisor carried no award dilution".
  - When `report.routeMetrics?.route === "reit"`, it appends to `routeMetrics.notes` (rendered by `sections.tsx:1121` and `markdown.ts:620`): "Priced under the REIT method before report spec {BRIDGE_METHOD_SINCE_SPEC_VERSION}: P/FFO, P/AFFO and the implied cap rate used price × weighted diluted shares and the block's own enterprise value, so they can differ from the multiples table" (FIN-311, FIN-108, FIN-119-V2).
  - (b) FIN-109: where `valuation.multiplesBasis` or `valuation.marketValueScope.enterpriseValueBasis` contains "is disclosed but its operating slice is not" and the stored lease amounts show the total is wholly a finance lease, it shows FIN-109's corrected sentence there and hides the stored "the FMP route publishes no split" entry from `appendix.missingData`.
  - FIN-407's display rule moved to Task 15b with Task 10 (B4b).
  - No figure is changed (D-67).

  - a table with one row per Part A finding (id, severity, fixed in task, test file, stored-report impact);
  - the owner decisions OD-1, OD-3, OD-4, OD-6, OD-7 and OD-8, with sources, and the pre-flight rulings they took (I1, I2, I3, I6, I7, the one-basis median and the look-back order);
  - the replay table from Task 21, with the band moves of Task 21 Step 5;
  - "lost evidence" (I9): the post-integration scripts and the Feldstein page images were lost on 2026-10-28; Feldstein is cited by its citation, not re-checked, and every figure was re-measured from this plan's own steps;
  - "not fixed / residual": WACC capital-structure weights ignore temporary equity; the debt-like lines' tax relief is the D-98 marginal-rate floor (21% US federal, state and local taxes excluded; 15% elsewhere) applied as if each claim were deductible against taxable income when paid, so a non-deductible payment (government fines and penalties, 26 U.S.C. §162(f)), one that only adds to tax basis (contingent consideration: the acquired assets' basis, amortised over 15 years for intangibles, or the acquired shares' basis, recovered only on a sale) or one made by an issuer with no taxable income to absorb it is understated by up to rate × amount, and the omitted state tax understates the relief (known issue B2b-1 is otherwise resolved: the lines are after tax); a pension deficit's `net × (1 − rate)` is its after-tax cost if funded at once (Feldstein 1982, p. 78), and the bridge does not model a slower funding schedule, which costs more in present value on his assumptions; a rate-regulated utility (SIC 4911, 4923, 4924, 4931, 4932 or 4941) has its pension and OPEB deficit disclosed, not deducted, on the assumption that it is recovered through rates (ASC 980), because the offsetting regulatory asset is reported only in dimensional XBRL, which companyfacts does not carry (DUK keeps main's treatment; a utility that cannot recover its deficit is overstated by it); netting the deficit against the pension regulatory asset once dimensional XBRL is available is routed to the free-data-source batch (pre-flight I7); parent temporary equity that is redeemable COMMON stock already in the diluted count (ESOP puts, SPAC Class A) would be counted twice, because companyfacts do not separate it from redeemable preferred (disclosed in the bridge note, Task 2); pension and the other new obligations not added to the market EV; where a provider-route run's temporary-equity enrichment was withheld at the bridge date, a redeemable claim may be missing from the bridge and the EVs, which keep main's figures with a warn gap (addendum 2, item 7); a vendor row whose CIK differs from the confirmed companyfacts skips the enrichment with a gap that fires for every issuer, so it is not read as a sign of a missing claim (`financialEnrichment.ts:903`). (The convertible and equity-method residuals are B4b's, Task 19b.)
  - The behaviour changes a reader will notice beyond the figures: forecast EPS and P/FCF withheld for issuers with temporary equity (as for permanent preferred; already withheld for UNH and WMT by their book NCI); a redeemable NCI with no profit key, or beside a null book NCI (I2), withholds the per share under E-5; temporary equity now lowers UNH's and WMT's DCF values (about −$1.56 and −$0.04) and UNH's ROIC (about −0.22pp); every retirement and environmental line (E-7) is now deducted after tax, so each issuer that tags one has a higher DCF value than before by the relief; a covered operating lease leaves sales-to-capital's invested capital, which raises lessees' values (AMZN #51 about +6.9%); and issuers with a large recognised pension and OPEB deficit fall the most (in the census, CAT by about $6.56 and BA by about $5.87 net a share, JNJ by about $4.25 with its litigation accrual and earn-out, VZ by about $2.23; Task 6 Step 5).

### Task 8: An equity-method stake is valued once: its income placement is read, not presumed (FIN-401, FIN-401-V1; B4b, redesigned per pre-flight C1, I4, I5)

**Files:**
- Modify `src/edgar/financialEnrichment.ts:854` at B13 (`admitVendorLongTermInvestments`, R0's provider-path stakes fill; `:754-790` at `main`): it takes the total through the builder's `longTermInvestments` chain with its floor, not through its own `firstInstant(tagsFor("longTermInvestments"))` copy (pre-flight I5).

No edit to the `longTermInvestments` chain itself (`:1079-1110` at B13).

B13's `lineItemChain` copies `floorsFor("longTermInvestments")` onto the chain's `first` step (B13 Task 1), and B13's `collectTags` collects the floor tag. Do NOT add a `ChainSpec` field: B13's `first.unlessBelow` is the one mechanism (master plan shared-files row 10). Its semantics, which this task relies on: each synonym is checked on its own against the same period's floor value with `SYNONYM_FLOOR_TOLERANCE = { relative: 0.005, absolute: 2_000_000 }`; the floor probe runs inside `notes.quietly`; a skipped synonym adds the note "`<label>: LongTermInvestments <value> not used — below the same period's EquityMethodInvestments <value>, so it is not the total`"; when every synonym is skipped the step returns null and records a refusal, which becomes a withholding only if the field ends null, so here (the piece sum serves) it stays a note. `PRETAX_EXCLUDING_EQUITY_METHOD_TAG` (`tagSynonyms.ts:98-99` at B13, gate `:197`) is reused for the placement test; no new constant.

S is the whole LTI when its `fieldProvenance.longTermInvestments.tags` name only equity-method pieces; otherwise `min(LTI, EquityMethodInvestments)` read at the bridge date with `instantFact` (`src/pipeline/stageB/marketableEquity.ts`) under the run's observed-at cutoff; otherwise 0).

- Test: `tests/edgar.equityMethodPlacement.audit20261027.test.ts`, `tests/stageB.equityMethodStake.audit20261027.test.ts`.

**Interfaces:**
- Produces on income rows: `equityMethodIncomePlacement?: { placement: "in-operating-income" | "below-operating-income" | "undetermined" | "immaterial"; income: number | null; basis: string }`. The field is absent when no equity-method income is filed or derivable for the period and no stake question arises. `"immaterial"` is a filed income within the placement tolerance of 0 (a filed 0 included): the quarter is measured, its income is the filed amount, and it takes the window's placement (pre-flight I4).
- **One long-term-investments chain on both routes (pre-flight I5).** The builder's `longTermInvestments` chain, with the floor declared below (B13's `unlessBelow`, checked by `belowComponentFloor`, `src/edgar/synonymFloor.ts:22` at B13), is evaluated for one instant by one exported function that both the builder's balance rows and `admitVendorLongTermInvestments` call (export B13's single-instant evaluation if it exists; otherwise add `export function longTermInvestmentsChainAt(facts, date, unit, observedAt)` beside the chain in `statements.ts` and have the builder use it too). The fill re-lists no tag and copies no floor.
- Produces `DcfAssumptionInputs.equityMethodStake?: { value: number; treatment: "added-back" | "operating"; incomePlacement: "in-operating-income" | "below-operating-income" | "undetermined"; basis: string } | null`.
- Produces house constant `EQUITY_METHOD_PLACEMENT_TOLERANCE = { relative: 0.001, absolute: 1_000_000 }` (statements.ts; recorded in DECISIONS as a project choice).

Placement rule (the algorithm the tests pin):

```ts
// Filed OperatingIncomeLoss, E = filed IncomeLossFromEquityMethodInvestments:
//   |E| <= tol (a filed 0 included)  -> "immaterial", income E   (pre-flight I4: a filed quarter; takes the window's placement)
//   otherwise, rest = pretax - OI - below + interest (InterestExpense or InterestAndDebtExpense; 0 when neither is filed), with
//     the COMPONENT path: NonoperatingIncomeExpense not filed; below = OtherNonoperatingIncomeExpense + InvestmentIncomeInterest
//     the AGGREGATE path: NonoperatingIncomeExpense filed; below = that aggregate
//   component path, |rest| <= tol    -> "in-operating-income", income E
//   aggregate path, |rest| <= tol    -> "undetermined"  (pre-flight C1: the aggregate can hold E, as CMCSA's, DUK's and NEE's do,
//                                        so rest ~ 0 cannot show where E sits; main's treatment is kept for the stake)
//   either path, |rest - E| <= tol   -> "below-operating-income", income E
//   otherwise                        -> "undetermined"  (treated as below: Rule 5-03 default; note says "assumed")
// Derived EBIT, as B13 builds it: pretax (the incomeBeforeTax chain: the total, or the B-32 domestic + foreign
//   composition, which counts as a STANDARD pretax below) + interest (absent when B13's FIN-507/FIN-508 rule finds
//   the interest inside the NonoperatingIncomeExpense aggregate) - adjustments (B13's list, with
//   OtherNonoperatingIncomeExpense componentOf the aggregate). Read what was subtracted from the discloseAdjusted
//   context's `amounts` (B13 Task 5: each subtracted item with its amount), never from the disclosure text and
//   never assuming an interest tag in the provenance. A filed OperatingIncomeLoss withheld for presentation
//   (B13 `stopOnRefusal`) is not re-derived, so such a row carries no placement field.
//   `amounts` holds a subtracted IncomeLossFromEquityMethodInvestments                           -> "below-operating-income", income E
//   base tag is PRETAX_EXCLUDING_EQUITY_METHOD_TAG, E filed,
//     |Revenues - CostsAndExpenses - pretax| <= tol                       -> "in-operating-income", income E
//   base tag is PRETAX_EXCLUDING_EQUITY_METHOD_TAG otherwise              -> "below-operating-income" (definition)
//   standard pretax tag, no E filed for the period, and the issuer files
//     EquityMethodInvestments at the period end                           -> "in-operating-income", income null
// Derived fourth quarter (fiscal year less nine months; pre-flight I4): the rule above runs on the quarter's derived
//   operands (OI, pretax, below, interest and E, each FY - nine months), every pair chosen by B13's oneBasisPair
//   (src/edgar/presentationLineage.ts:180 at B13) exactly as B13 derives the quarter's other lines. When any operand's
//   pair is withheld or not derivable, the quarter's placement is "undetermined".
// tol = max(relative * |pretax|, absolute)
```

DCF consequence (the window rule, pre-flight I4): the base window is measured "in-operating-income" when every quarter (or the fiscal year) reads "in-operating-income" with a measured income or "immaterial", and at least one reads "in-operating-income". Then the EBIT seed and each such history year subtract the summed filed incomes (an immaterial quarter adds its filed amount, 0 for a filed 0), and the stake is added back (`treatment: "added-back"`). If any base period is "in-operating-income" with `income: null`, the stake stays in operating capital and is not added back (`treatment: "operating"`). Any "undetermined" quarter, a mixed window, or a window of "immaterial" quarters only keeps `main`'s behaviour, with the note worded per placement.

- Modify `src/pipeline/stageB/valuation.ts:2300-2332` (`DcfRunOptions` unchanged; `DcfBridge.convertibles`), `:5650-5715` (`ValuationBundleInputs.convertibles`), about `:6270-6300` (after the base `runDcf`, enumerate the readings, re-run, keep the lowest, and set `runOpts` to it so sensitivity, growth x margin, reverse DCF and scenarios inherit it), `:4689` (`ExcessReturnInputs.convertibles`), `:5267`, `:5273-5341` (excess-return per share, start alternative and start/CoE grid on the chosen reading).
- Modify `src/pipeline/stageB/projections.ts:162-180` (`interestRunRate` also returns `ratePct`) and `:695-731` (a converted note's interest leaves the run-rate; the assumption line names the reading).
- Modify `src/pipeline/stageB/fairValue.ts:502-511` and `src/report/auditTables.ts:90-110` (a bridge row per instrument: "Diluted shares less N conversion shares of <instrument> (counted as its claim)" or "Plus <instrument> treated as converted C").
- Test: `tests/stageB.convertibles.audit20261027.test.ts`.

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  ```ts
  export interface ConvertibleInstrument { kind: "notes" | "preferred"; conversionShares: number; claim: number | null;
    settlement: "full-shares" | "net-share" | "unknown"; conversionPrice: number | null;
    period: { start: string; end: string }; basis: string }
  export interface ConvertibleReading { kind: "notes" | "preferred"; conversionShares: number; claim: number | null;
    reading: "claim" | "converted" | "net-share" | "unresolved"; basis: string }
  // "unresolved": claim unreadable or settlement unknown; claim and N both kept (HEAD's figure, the lowest)
  export function convertibleInstrumentsAt(facts: CompanyFacts | null | undefined,
    divisor: { end: string; basis: "quarter" | "annual" }, bridgeDate: string, unit: string, observedAt: string,
    bridge: { permanentPreferred: number | null; totalDebt: number | null }):
    { instruments: ConvertibleInstrument[]; convertiblePreferredClaim: number | null; gaps: ManifestEntry[] }
  export function convertibleVariants(instruments: readonly ConvertibleInstrument[],
    deducted: { preferred: number; notes: number }):
    { converted: Array<"notes" | "preferred">; restored: { notes: number; preferred: number }; sharesRemoved: number }[]
  ```
  `convertibleVariants` enumerates every subset of the instruments that have a known claim AND `settlement === "full-shares"`; every other instrument (unknown claim, `net-share`, `unknown` settlement) keeps its claim and its shares in every variant, i.e. `main`'s figure for that instrument. **`restored` never exceeds what the bridge deducted for the instrument (pre-flight C2):** `restored.preferred ≤ min(C, deducted.preferred)` and `restored.notes ≤ min(C, deducted.notes)`; notes whose claim exceeds the bridge row's total debt are not inside the bridge's debt (D-95) and read "unresolved".
- `convertiblePreferredClaim` is the filed `ConvertiblePreferredStockNonredeemableOrRedeemableIssuerOptionValue` at the bridge date (including a series issued in the period: a 0 or absent value at the prior balance date does not stop it), or null. valueCompany deducts it under C2's rule: added to the permanent preferred when the row's `preferredStock` is null or 0; the larger of the two (never the sum) when both are positive, with the warn gap `valuation.dcf.preferredClaimOverlap` naming both amounts (`millionsPair`).
- Produces `DcfBridge.convertibles?: ConvertibleReading[]`, `ExcessReturnResult.convertibles?: ConvertibleReading[]`, and manifest fields `valuation.perShare.convertibleClaim` (warn: N filed, claim unreadable or not established at the bridge date; it names the instrument and prints the bound N/(D − N)), `valuation.perShare.convertibleShares` (info: convertible tagged, no increment filed for the period) and `valuation.dcf.preferredClaimOverlap` (warn, C2).
- Reader rules:
  - N comes from `IncrementalCommonSharesAttributableToConversionOfDebtSecurities` / `…OfPreferredStock` (unit `shares`), with `end === divisor.end` and a duration of 80-100 days for a quarter or 350-380 days for a year (a six- or nine-month year-to-date fact is never read), in 10-Q or 10-K forms filed by `observedAt`.
  - The notes claim is the bridge-date carrying amount. `ConvertibleNotesPayable` is an inclusive total (current portion included; `INCLUSIVE_DEBT_COMPONENT_TAGS`, `statements.ts:528` at B13), so when it is filed it is the claim on its own. Otherwise the claim is the first filed of `ConvertibleDebtNoncurrent`, `ConvertibleLongTermNotesPayable` plus the first of `ConvertibleDebtCurrent`, `ConvertibleNotesPayableCurrent` (`tagSynonyms.ts:498`, `:509` at B13). Never inclusive total + current.
  - Settlement (OD-2): preferred is `full-shares`. Notes are `full-shares` only when an undimensioned `DebtInstrumentConvertibleConversionPrice1` K (unit `USD/shares` in the model currency) is filed at or within 366 days before the bridge date by `observedAt` and |N − C/K| ≤ 0.05 × C/K; `net-share` when N < 0.95 × C/K (conversion-spread shares only, ASC 260-10-45-40(b)); `unknown` otherwise (no K, or N above 1.05 × C/K). `net-share` adds an info note; `unknown` adds the warn gap `valuation.perShare.convertibleClaim` with the N/(D − N) bound.
  - The preferred claim C is the filed `ConvertiblePreferredStockNonredeemableOrRedeemableIssuerOptionValue` at the bridge date, and the bridge deducts it (C2, above). With no filed carrying value of the instrument at the bridge date, the claim is not established: the instrument keeps `main`'s figure (nothing restored, N kept in the divisor) and the warn gap `valuation.perShare.convertibleClaim` names it. The plan's earlier fallback to the bridge's whole preferred deduction is removed (pre-flight C2): companyfacts do not split preferred by series, so that deduction may not hold the convertible series.
  - The published reading is the variant with the lowest per share; ties go to the claim reading.

The sensitivity grid's centre cell equals `dcf.perShare`, and `dcf.bridge.convertibles[0].reading` is `"converted"` or `"claim"` accordingly.
  - `it("the excess-return route applies the same reading")`: `excessReturnModel({ …bank inputs, dilutedShares: 110, convertibles: [notes N 10, C 50, settlement "full-shares"] })` gives `perShare` `toBeCloseTo(Math.min((Eq + 50) / 110, Eq / 100), 9)`, with `Eq` the model's `equityValue`, and `startCoeGrid` centre equal to `perShare`.
  - `it("forecast EPS follows the reading")`: on the claim reading, `epsBase[0]` divides by `D − N`. On the converted reading for notes, the interest in the assumption line falls by `C × ratePct / 100`, and the line matches `/convertible notes treated as converted/`.
  - Control `it("no increment filed leaves the bridge unchanged")`: no instruments give the HEAD `perShare` and `bridge` deep-equal.

## Review notes

The three sections below (Review notes, Integration notes, Post-integration review) are history. Where they differ from the plan above, "Pre-flight amendments (2026-10-29)" and the text it changed are the authority: in particular the stamp values, the "nothing moves" premises, the replay figures, the `B4-post-int/` evidence (lost 2026-10-28) and Tasks 8 and 11's designs.

Independent review of 2026-10-27 against `findings.json`, the 21 probes (read, not run), the code at HEAD 6542531 and the cited sources (each owner decision's key source fetched). Coverage: all 21 B4 findings are fixed by a task; none is skipped or disputed. AI-203 and AI-401-V2 are not B4 ids. Changes made to this plan:

1. **Spec reference.** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-27-audit-remediation-decisions.md` does not exist at HEAD; the header now says so.
2. **Global Constraints.** `tests/lease.numericConsumers.test.ts:163-180` was listed as a defect pin; it pins the FCX-1 refusal path this batch keeps, so it must pass unchanged. The REIT defect pin is `tests/stageB.valuation.test.ts:2489-2502`.
3. **DECISIONS.** The plan said both "each decision its own entry" and "one entry"; it is now one D-next entry with one bullet per decision (repository practice).
4. **OD-1.**
   - The claims now reach every "senior to common" guard: forecast EPS, the P/FCF cash-ownership guard and the own-history allocation checks.
   - The parent part is labelled "temporary equity attributable to the parent", because the tag also holds redeemable common shares (residual recorded).
   - The Deloitte 9.4 quote was verified and replaced with the page's own wording.
5. **OD-2: new rule for notes that settle their principal in cash.**
   - Under ASC 260-10-45-40(b), such a note adds only spread shares, so HEAD's figure is already right for it, and either B4 reading would overstate value. The plan applied the two readings to every note.
   - Notes now get the two readings only when a filed conversion price shows N is the full conversion count; otherwise they read as `net-share` or `unresolved`, which keep HEAD's lower figure and add a disclosure.
   - Sources corrected: the Equity Methods page does not name ASU 2020-06; the IB Interview Questions quote was not verbatim. A cash-principal source was added.
6. **OD-3.** The CBIZ quotes are now verbatim.
7. **OD-4.**
   - The plan said E-7's "conventions unchanged", but it does change E-7's "current portion alone is not the obligation" rule for two families. That revision is now stated, in OD-4 and in Task 20.
   - Postemployment benefits are answered explicitly.

   - The FE "Debt Equivalents" page does not mention earn-outs; Hectelion's "earn-outs due" now carries that support.
   - IRC 4980 is 20%, or 50% without a replacement plan (was "50%").
8. **OD-5 and OD-6.**
   - S-X 5-03's "may be presented in a different position" proviso is added. It is why departures must be detected.
   - The Damodaran cross-holdings PDF cannot be read for verification; it is replaced with his "Holdings in Other Firms" page, quoted.

9. **OD-8.** Deloitte 9.4 does not mention OP units (whole page checked). The OP-unit treatment is now attributed to filer practice.
10. **Task 1.**
    - The runStageB test asserted `valuationDate.basis`, but `currencyBundle`'s quote timestamp is 2025-07-05, so `valuationDate` is undefined and the assertion would throw at HEAD and after the fix. It is removed; the roll-forward wording is pinned by the unit test.

11. **Task 2.**
    - The fold now happens at the top of `valueCompany`, before the E-5 block. The plan's "before building runOpts" would have bypassed E-5 for redeemable NCI.
    - The unsplit check now uses `dcf.bridge.noncontrollingDeductionBasis`.
    - The runStageB test now compares the EV-to-equity gap. As written, it would have failed once Task 3 changed invested capital for one caption only.
    - Added tests for redeemable NCI with no profit key, and for forecast-EPS/P/FCF withholding.
12. **Task 4.**
    - `establishedSalesToCapital` is now exported with an explicit signature. The history ratio shows in the basis only when the floor binds, so the history tests could not observe it otherwise; they are now concrete.
    - Added a grep step for other unproven-row fixtures.
13. **Task 5.**
    - The history test is now concrete.
    - `Infinity` must never reach `clampWithNote` or the basis text.

    - The HEAD failure point is 699 → 700, not 699 → 701.
14. **Task 6.**
    - The new `DebtLikeObligations` members are optional. As required members, they would have broken the existing E-7 test literals under `tsc`.
    - The "not added to the market EV" sentence is appended only when a line is deducted, in `valueCompany`. `multiplesFramework` never sees companyfacts, and an unconditional sentence would move every report's EV basis.
15. **Task 7.**
    - The predecessor read is now per family. The whole-object rule would drop the predecessor's ARO whenever the successor's first 10-Q tagged one unrelated family.
    - The wiring test uses a fixture-scale ARO; the XOM amount exceeds the bundle's EV.

16. **Task 8.**
    - The mechanism for the LTI-chain floor is named: an `unlessBelow` field on the `first` chain step.
    - Test amounts are scaled (`5 * M`).
    - The XOM replay is in equity value.
17. **Task 9.** It is stated that fixture mutations are absolute (`currencyBundle` scales rows when it builds them, and `weightedAverageShsOut` is scaled).
18. **Task 10.** Added a weighted-class (1,500-unit) test. The probe's equal-units fixture cannot tell "filter the info entry" from "divide by the economic count", which the finding says must land together.
19. **Task 11.**
    - The settlement rule from item 5 is added.
    - `ConvertibleNotesPayable` is an inclusive total, so it is never added to a current tag.
    - The two-instrument expectation was arithmetically wrong. The plan had `1040/105`; restoring the notes keeps their 10 shares, so the right figure is `1040/110`.
    - The test literals now carry `settlement`.
20. **Task 12.**
    - Removing `netDebt` and four other fields from `ReitInputs` breaks `tsc` in five test files the plan did not list; they are now listed with replacements that keep their figures.
    - `tests/stageB.financialValuation.test.ts:418-419` pins P/FFO 12.5 on a 50 × 100 market cap beside a 10,000 quote. That pin encodes FIN-311, and it is re-pinned to 25 and 33.33 with a comment.
    - The market-cap expression and the guard conditions are concrete. No field is added to the serialized `EnterpriseValueBridge`.
21. **Task 14.** At main, the EV-agreement line passes, because both sides omit the claims. A "+70 M" assertion makes it fail there. The run against main now uses the Task 21 archive (a git worktree has no `node_modules`).
22. **Task 15 (rewritten).**
    - The legacy rule now compares against a frozen `BRIDGE_METHOD_SINCE_SPEC_VERSION`. Comparing against `REPORT_SPEC_VERSION` would have marked B4-method reports legacy as soon as B5 bumped that constant.
    - The rule is idempotent: the chain runs twice on the `parseStoredReportWithSafety` path.
    - Target fields are concrete (`fairValue.basis`, `routeMetrics.notes`).
    - The file list is corrected: `correctedCli.ts` renders through `printHtml`, `presentation.ts` reads only market data, and `legacyEntitySafety.ts` is added.
    - Added a FIN-407 display rule (display-only finding).
    - Added a pointer to the Next.js server/client components guide.
23. **Task 16.** `docs/METHODOLOGY.md:2231-2232` (`:2391-2392` at B13's `3ca1c6b`) names the two method stamps. Each stamp now gets its history comment line, plus the since-version assertion.
24. **Task 17.** The payload-golden expectation now matches the conditional EV-basis sentence.
25. **Task 19.** The residuals list is extended, along with the reader-visible behaviour changes.
26. **Task 21.**
    - Per-side working directories give the `@/` alias the right `src`.
    - An explicit ban on `research:replay --hash/--job`, which reads the live database.
    - (A splice error made during this review was repaired; the step list is intact.)
27. **Task 22.** Verify runs in a clean worktree. The owner's untracked `docs/independent-audit-2026-10-19/*.ts` fails `tsc` in the main checkout, per the audit baseline.

Left for the main session:
- **Spec file.** Write `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-27-audit-remediation-decisions.md`, or drop the reference from all 13 plans.
- **Tax convention.** Practitioners (FE Training, verified) deduct pension deficits after tax. This batch keeps E-7's before-tax convention for all five debt-like families and discloses it. Resolving known-issue B2b-1 for every line at once, at the D-98 marginal rate, is the better-supported follow-up and needs the owner's E-7 revisited.
- **Filed conversion prices.** How often companyfacts carry an undimensioned `DebtInstrumentConvertibleConversionPrice1` is unmeasured. If it is rare, most notes will read `unresolved` (HEAD's figure with a warn gap), and the High fix lands mainly for convertible preferred.
- **Redeemable common stock in parent temporary equity** (ESOP puts, SPAC Class A) cannot be separated from redeemable preferred in companyfacts; recorded as a residual.
- **Merge order and renumbering.** Merge after B1/B2. Renumber D-next, the stamps and `BRIDGE_METHOD_SINCE_SPEC_VERSION` together at merge. B3 re-pins the payload golden if it merges second. B5, B6, B9, B10 and B12 rebase onto this batch's `valuation.ts`, `compute.ts`, `statements.ts`, `projections.ts` and `METHODOLOGY.md` changes, as the summary lists.
- `tmp/audit-2026-10-27/plans/B4.summary.json` predates this review; where they differ, this plan is the authority.

## Integration notes (2026-10-28)

Applied by the integration pass from `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-27-audit-remediation-decisions.md` section 4.3 ("Main-session resolutions (2026-10-28)", binding), and from the B13 plan's cross-batch notes (B13 merges before this batch; master plan queue slots 4 and 5, shared-files rows 5 and 10). The "Review notes" above are kept as history: their "Left for the main session" items on the tax convention, the filed conversion prices and redeemable common stock are settled here. Each change and the 4.3 line (or cross-batch note) it implements:

1. **Spec line and branch line.** The decisions spec now exists; the Spec line points to its sections 2.4 and 4.3 and drops "this file does not exist". The branch is cut after B13 merges (master plan queue). Implements: the brief's "fix the plan's Spec line"; master plan queue slot 5.
2. **OD-4, tax convention (4.3 B4: "Deduct pension and OPEB deficits, and the other debt-like families, after tax at the D-98 marginal rate, revising E-7").** All five families (asset-retirement, environmental, pension and OPEB, loss contingencies, contingent consideration) are deducted at `net × (1 − rate)`, with `rate = assumptions.investmentTax.ratePct` (the D-98 / D-58 marginal-rate floor: 21% US federal, 15% elsewhere). The relief is applied to the amount deducted (after a settlement-fund offset), with one netted line per claim, labels "(filed, after tax at {rate}%…)" and a closing note that states the approximation (no state tax, deduction assumed when paid, §162(f) fines and capitalised earn-outs credited a relief they will not earn). Before tax is now a rejected alternative, and so is a separate positive tax-relief line (the bridge surfaces read only a component's sign). Revises E-7 / D-104 and resolves known issue B2b-1 (`docs/audit/2026-10-22-audit-batch2b-valuation-mediums.md:55`, left as history). Sources added: Feldstein (NBER c11396, 1982; the scanned PDF could not be text-extracted on 2026-10-28, so it is quoted from an OCR or the printed volume before DECISIONS cites it, or cited as secondary), IB Interview Questions on pensions (quoted, checked 2026-10-28), 26 U.S.C. §404(a) and §162(f)(1) (checked 2026-10-28), and D-98's rate sentence. The FE Training quote is kept and is no longer flagged as a deviation.
3. **Task 6 tests and implementation (same 4.3 line).** The new file's expectations are recomputed after tax: the three new families lower equity by 154.09 × 0.85 = 130.9765 at the fixture's 15% (no domicile), with each component exact (−78.03, −46.75, −6.1965), and by 154.09 × 0.79 = 121.7311 in a new US-domicile test. The existing E-7 pins in `tests/stageB.debtLikeObligations.audit20261022.test.ts` are re-pinned, each with a comment naming the revised owner convention: −586 → −498.1; −5.86 → −4.981 a share; labels "(filed, after tax at 15%…)"; fixed-bridge text "…after tax (balance at 2025-09-30) 326.40, … after tax 171.70"; −9.5 → −8.075; −200 → −170; −150 → −127.5. The fully offset ARO stays a zero deduction. `src/report/scenarioPresentation.ts` (`name()` keeps "after tax") joins the task's files. Review Focus item 6 is added (the relief applies to the amount deducted, never to the offset). Docs: METHODOLOGY's E-7 sentences at `:988`, `:1016` and `:1025-1026` take the after-tax wording.
4. **Replay expectations (same 4.3 line).** Task 6 Step 5: JNJ about −$2.0 (was −$2.6), FCX pension 0.725B after tax, every E-7 issuer up by 21% of its line (BA +≈$0.26). Task 7: the successor wiring test expects −98.75 M (125 M less 21%: `currencyBundle` has a US profile), and XOM's predecessor ARO deducts 9.889B, so ≈760.39B → ≈750.50B. Task 21's table carries these rows plus one row per E-7 issuer. (The JNJ, XOM and BA figures here left the census pension deficits out; they are superseded by "Post-integration review (2026-10-28)", item 3.)
5. **DECISIONS and docs tasks (same 4.3 line).** Task 20's OD-4 bullet records the revised tax convention, who decided it and what it supersedes. Its house-constants line says the D-98 rate is reused (no new rate), and Step 2's grep list adds the before-tax sentences. Task 19's residuals replace "tax-effecting all debt-like lines" with the after-tax approximation residual, and the reader-visible changes add the higher value of every E-7 issuer. Task 15's legacy note (a) adds, with a test, that stored reports deducted retirement and environmental obligations before tax. Task 16's Stage B paragraph says so too. The PR (Task 24) names the change and the decision.
6. **Undimensioned conversion prices measured before the note wording is fixed (4.3 B4: "B4 measures it offline over the 45 census snapshots' companyfacts before choosing the note wording").** Task 11 gains Step 0: a read-only script over `tmp/metis-audit-2026-10-22/scratch/snapshots/main/*.json` (the snapshots' own companyfacts; no SEC call), its expected output, and a fixed decision rule between two exact wordings (W-rare, expected, and W-common). The expected output is what the integration pass measured on 2026-10-28: no issuer files K within a year of its balance date, three filed one before 2020, DUK's notes increment has no K, GOOGL and PG file convertible-preferred increments, and SNOW tags convertible debt with no increment. The in-report warn-gap text is fixed and does not depend on the measurement. OD-2 records the measurement, Task 11 Step 5 and Task 21 name GOOGL, PG, DUK and SNOW, and Tasks 19 and 20 carry the counts.
7. **Redeemable common stock in parent temporary equity (4.3 B4: "redeemable common stock inside parent temporary equity stays a disclosed residual").** It stays a residual, now disclosed in the report itself. Task 2's bridge note adds a sentence whenever a parent or unsplit temporary-equity claim is deducted, with a test and a control. OD-1, METHODOLOGY (Task 2 Step 6), Task 19 and Task 20 say so.
8. **B13 cross-batch: the long-term-investments floor (B13 Review notes, "B4 (merges after)", first bullet; master plan row 10).** Task 8 no longer adds a `ChainSpec` field. It declares the floor on the `longTermInvestments` synonym entry through B13's third `entry()` argument, so B13's `lineItemChain` copies it and B13's `collectTags` collects it. It relies on B13's semantics: per-synonym checks, `SYNONYM_FLOOR_TOLERANCE = { relative: 0.005, absolute: 2_000_000 }`, the quiet probe, and a refusal that stays a note. The 271-vs-32,653 test now also pins B13's note text and the absence of a withholding. A within-tolerance control (32,600) is added, and the 40,000 control stays. `src/edgar/tagSynonyms.ts` is in the commit.
9. **B13 cross-batch: the derived-EBIT context (same B13 notes, bullets 2-4).** Task 8's placement rule reads the `amounts` in B13's `discloseAdjusted` context, never the disclosure text. It treats the B-32 domestic + foreign pretax composition as a standard pretax, does not assume an interest tag (FIN-507 and FIN-508 may omit it), and gives no placement to a filed operating income that B13 withholds for presentation (`stopOnRefusal`). After B13 moves `:1690-1700`, the edit location is found again by `notWhenBaseTag`. A rebase-check test on a B13-shaped derived EBIT is added (aggregate filed, no interest element, equity-method income subtracted; plus the B-32 composition variant), and Step 4 re-runs B13's two suites.
10. **B13 cross-batch: one comparator and the wrapper order (same B13 notes, bullet 5; decisions spec 2.15; master plan row 5).** Task 15 no longer adds `compareSpecVersion` to `schema.ts`: it imports B3's `specVersionBefore`, and stops if it is missing. `withLegacyBridgeMethod` is wired after B13's `withLegacyEdgarDerivedLines` (itself after B3's wrapper) at the five sites, and its line follows B13's in `fairValue.basis`. A composition test shows the B3, B13 and B4 corrections once each on every surface. Task 16's stamp assertion uses `specVersionBefore`.
11. **B13 cross-batch: the replay baseline (same B13 notes, bullet 6; master plan row 10).** Task 21 rebuilds the census statement rows from each snapshot's companyfacts with each side's builder, using B13 Task 16's options. Task 8's builder changes then reach the replay, and B13's moves (JNJ FIN-509, LLY, MCD, BAC) sit in the post-B13 `main` baseline. The JNJ, XOM and BA baselines are re-measured on it; Task 8 Step 5 says which B13 floors could move XOM and BA. The evidence inputs and Realty Income stay on stored rows.
12. **Risk-manifest placement (consistency with B13's Global Constraints and master plan row 47).** `src/report/legacyBridgeMethod.ts` is outside the core include, so Task 15 adds it to `RISK_SOURCE_MANIFEST` and `EXPECTED_RISK_SOURCES` after B13's file, and Task 22 Step 3 holds it to the risk floors. Without this, `tests/risk.coverage.test.ts` would fail `test:product`.

Not changed: the other 4.3 lines concern other batches. OD-1 changes only by the in-report disclosure of item 7, and OD-3 and OD-5 to OD-8 are unchanged in substance (OD-5 now names B13's floor as its mechanism, item 8). The Review notes' merge-order line ("Merge after B1/B2") is superseded by item 1. The B4 planner summary (`tmp/audit-2026-10-27/plans/B4.summary.json`) was updated to match this plan; where they differ, this plan is the authority.

## Post-integration review (2026-10-28)

An independent review of the after-tax change to the debt-like families (OD-4, Task 6 and every value it re-pins, Task 7's wiring value, the replay expectations, Task 11 Step 0) and of the open line the decisions spec gives this plan (O-4). It was traced through the code at HEAD 6542531 (`debtLikeObligations.ts`, the bridge block of `valueCompany` at `valuation.ts:6205-6244`, `marginalTaxFloor`, `fixedBridgeNonOperatingText`, `computeDcfDisplay`, the keyless profile at `keyless.ts:1573`) and the E-7 test file. Every measurement below was read-only and offline, from the census snapshots' own companyfacts; no SEC, provider or model call was made, no database was opened, and no test suite was run. Scripts and outputs were in `tmp/audit-2026-10-27/plans/B4-post-int/`, which was lost in the 2026-10-28 deletion (pre-flight I9: the figures are re-measured by the pre-flight review and by this plan's own steps). Changes made to this plan:

1. **O-4 settled: Feldstein quoted from the chapter (decisions spec 4.5).** The NBER PDF has no text layer, so its page images were rendered offline and read. OD-4 now quotes pages 77-78 verbatim: pension payments "are tax deductible while the principal repayments on a bond are not", and "it is important to measure pension obligations in terms of their net-of-tax cost". It also quotes the page-78 example, in which funding a deficit at once costs the pretax-discounted claim times one minus the rate, which is exactly `net × (1 − rate)`. Feldstein is now a primary source. Task 20 copies the quotes after checking them against the two page images kept untracked beside the scripts. The decision did not rest on him, and it is unchanged. The IB Interview Questions formula was re-fetched and matches.
2. **OD-4 and the closing note, made exact.** The relief assumes a deduction "against taxable income" (an issuer with no taxable income earns it late or never). Contingent consideration "only adds to tax basis": the acquired assets' basis, amortised over 15 years for intangibles, or the acquired shares' basis, recovered only on a sale. The plan's "deductible only over time" missed the stock-deal case, in which nothing is deducted while the shares are held. Two sources were added: 26 U.S.C. §461(h)(2)(C) (a tort liability's economic performance "occurs as the payments to such person are made") and a practitioner summary of the buyer's earn-out basis. OD-4 also states what the relief means for a pension deficit (the after-tax cost of funding it at once) and records the funding-schedule residual. Task 19 adds that residual and the ASC 980 utility residual (DUK).
3. **Replay expectations corrected (Task 6 Step 5, Task 7 Step 5, Task 8 Step 5, Task 21).** The plan expected JNJ about −$2.0, BA ≈ $33.33 and XOM ≈ 750.50B, and "PEP and others by small amounts". An emulation of Task 6's own reader over the 45 census snapshots shows that recognised pension and OPEB deficits reach the bridge for ten census issuers, two of them by about $6 a share:
   - **JNJ** ≈ −$4.25. Its 6.957B family-(b) deficit sits in the 10-K of 2025-12-28, which E-7's look-back reaches, beside the 5.5B loss accrual and the 0.729B earn-out.
   - **BA** ≈ $27.20 in total. Its family-(c) deficit is 6.135B at 2026-06-30.
   - **XOM** ≈ 743.34B. Its own 10-Q files a 9.068B deficit, which comes before the predecessor's ARO.
   - **Other pension deficits:** CAT ≈ −$6.40, VZ ≈ −$2.23, CVX ≈ −$1.57, PG ≈ −$0.96, DUK ≈ −$0.39, INTC ≈ −$0.10, FCX ≈ −$0.50.
   - **Loss accruals and earn-outs:** GOOGL ≈ −$1.11 (17.356B, current-only), V, MSFT, PEP.
   - **E-7 lines now after tax:** CVX gains ≈ +$1.71 from the new relief.
   - **Wrong attribution fixed:** Task 21 called the 9.625B ARO in the E-7 tests "FCX's". It is the DUK-shaped fixture, and DUK's ARO is fully offset by its decommissioning funds, so it earns no relief. FCX's ARO is 3.836B.
   - **Unchanged, with the reason stated:** BAC (excess-return route), LLY (pension older than its latest annual balance sheet), KO (earn-out outside the look-back), NVDA (a filed zero), AMZN #43 and MU #46 (nothing tagged). FCX's current-only 0.049B litigation accrual nets to zero against its same-date environmental accrual.
   - **What the replay must flag:** any B-29 domain failure the large pension lines trigger.
   - The Integration notes item 4 figures are marked superseded.
4. **Task 6 implementation text.** HEAD's closing note ends the accretion sentence with "the bridge does not adjust for either". Once the tax sentence is replaced, "either" has nothing to refer to, so the new sentence ends "for it", and HEAD's wording survives only on the no-rate branch. The fund-offset ("weighs") note now names the after-tax amount, and its re-pin asserts it. Task 6 Step 6 adds METHODOLOGY's "The bridge states both and adjusts for neither." (`:1020`) to the sentences that change.
5. **Re-pinned values recomputed and confirmed.**
   - The fixture's rate is 15% (`steady` sets no domicile; `marginalTaxFloor` gives the global minimum, and `fmtNum` prints "15").
   - −586 × 0.85 = −498.1; −4.981 a share on 100 shares; 384 × 0.85 = 326.40 and 202 × 0.85 = 171.70 in the fixed-bridge text, which `name()` builds as planned.
   - Fund offsets: 9.5 × 0.85 = 8.075 (the 961 ARO fully offset); (300 − 100) × 0.85 = 170; (300 − 150) × 0.85 = 127.5.
   - New families: 154.09 × 0.85 = 130.9765, components −78.03, −46.75 and −6.1965; 154.09 × 0.79 = 121.7311 against a same-domicile base. The same-domicile base is needed because the 21% floor also lifts the terminal tax from 20% to 21%.
   - Nothing else pins the old wording: a grep of `tests/`, `src/` and `docs/` for "(filed, before tax", "before any tax relief" and "debt-like-obligation" finds only the files the task names.
6. **Task 7's wiring value confirmed, and one test added.** `currencyBundle`'s profile is `country: "US"` (`tests/helpers/currencyBundle.ts:179`), and the census profiles carry "US" for US-incorporated issuers (`keyless.ts:1573`), so 21% applies and 125 M × 0.79 = −98.75 M. The new test "a family both registrants tag is read once, from the successor" (Review Focus 7) pins the XOM case: both registrants file a pension deficit. A summing or predecessor-first merge would deduct it twice or at the older balance.
7. **Task 11 Step 0 re-measured: identical.** n = 1 (DUK) and k = 0, so W-rare stands. The step now records the re-run.
8. **Task 15 (a) no longer claims a deduction a stored report did not make.** The legacy note said every pre-B4 DCF "deducted filed retirement and environmental obligations at their full amount". That is false for reports made before D-104, and for issuers that tagged none. The clause now appears only when the stored bridge holds a component labelled "(filed, before tax" (`valuation.dcf.bridge.nonOperatingComponents[].label`, which `computeDcfDisplay` stores). The test gains a control.
9. **Committed-markdown checks (Task 19).** The master plan commits this plan with the B4 PR, so Task 19 now adds it to `ALLOWED_MARKDOWN`, stages it by name and runs `tests/docs.lint.test.ts` beside the release test. The plan was checked read-only against `unsafePublicDocument`'s two patterns and `docs.lint`'s relative-link rule, before and after these edits: no home path, no credential-shaped value, no unresolved link.
